{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Uckh8JUAu9z3DXwj4CMTQK","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nStructured Pruning for Qwen2.5 architecture (GQA-group granularity).\\\\n\\\\nRemoves GQA groups (7 Q heads + 1 KV head each) and MLP channel groups\\\\nbased on RASP or standard importance scores.\\\\n\\\\nInput:  pretrained model + pruning scores\\\\nOutput: pruned model saved to disk\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimport argparse\\\\nimport copy\\\\nimport logging\\\\nimport os\\\\nimport random\\\\n\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nimport torch\\\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\\\n\\\\nlogging.basicConfig(\\\\n    level=logging.INFO,\\\\n    format=\\\\\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\\\\\",\\\\n)\\\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef set_seed(seed: int):\\\\n    random.seed(seed)\\\\n    np.random.seed(seed)\\\\n    torch.manual_seed(seed)\\\\n    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef prune_attention_heads(model, attn_keep_masks: dict):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Prune GQA groups from the model by slicing weight matrices in-place.\\\\n\\\\n    attn_keep_masks: dict[layer_idx -> list of GQA group indices to KEEP]\\\\n    Each GQA group = heads_per_kv Q heads + 1 KV head.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    config = model.config\\\\n    n_heads = config.num_attention_heads\\\\n    n_kv_heads = config.num_key_value_heads\\\\n    head_dim = config.hidden_size // n_heads\\\\n    heads_per_kv = n_heads // n_kv_heads\\\\n\\\\n    total_pruned_groups = 0\\\\n\\\\n    for layer_idx, keep_groups in attn_keep_masks.items():\\\\n        layer = model.model.layers[layer_idx]\\\\n        attn = layer.self_attn\\\\n        n_keep_groups = len(keep_groups)\\\\n        pruned_groups = n_kv_heads - n_keep_groups\\\\n\\\\n        # Expand GQA group indices to Q head weight indices\\\\n        q_indices = []\\\\n        for g in keep_groups:\\\\n            for qh in range(heads_per_kv):\\\\n                h = g * heads_per_kv + qh\\\\n                start = h * head_dim\\\\n                q_indices.extend(range(start, start + head_dim))\\\\n        q_indices = torch.tensor(q_indices, dtype=torch.long)\\\\n\\\\n        # KV head weight indices (1 per group)\\\\n        kv_indices = []\\\\n        for g in keep_groups:\\\\n            start = g * head_dim\\\\n            kv_indices.extend(range(start, start + head_dim))\\\\n        kv_indices = torch.tensor(kv_indices, dtype=torch.long)\\\\n\\\\n        # Slice q_proj: [n_heads*head_dim, hidden] -> [n_keep_q*head_dim, hidden]\\\\n        attn.q_proj.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(attn.q_proj.weight.data[q_indices])\\\\n        if attn.q_proj.bias is not None:\\\\n            attn.q_proj.bias = torch.nn.Parameter(attn.q_proj.bias.data[q_indices])\\\\n\\\\n        # Slice k_proj, v_proj: [n_kv*head_dim, hidden] -> [n_keep_kv*head_dim, hidden]\\\\n        attn.k_proj.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(attn.k_proj.weight.data[kv_indices])\\\\n        if attn.k_proj.bias is not None:\\\\n            attn.k_proj.bias = torch.nn.Parameter(attn.k_proj.bias.data[kv_indices])\\\\n\\\\n        attn.v_proj.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(attn.v_proj.weight.data[kv_indices])\\\\n        if attn.v_proj.bias is not None:\\\\n            attn.v_proj.bias = torch.nn.Parameter(attn.v_proj.bias.data[kv_indices])\\\\n\\\\n        # Slice o_proj: [hidden, n_heads*head_dim] -> [hidden, n_keep_q*head_dim]\\\\n        attn.o_proj.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(attn.o_proj.weight.data[:, q_indices])\\\\n\\\\n        # Update layer config\\\\n        n_keep_q = n_keep_groups * heads_per_kv\\\\n        attn.num_heads = n_keep_q\\\\n        attn.num_key_value_heads = n_keep_groups\\\\n        attn.head_dim = head_dim\\\\n\\\\n        total_pruned_groups += pruned_groups\\\\n\\\\n        if pruned_groups > 0:\\\\n            logger.info(\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"  Layer {layer_idx}: pruned {pruned_groups}/{n_kv_heads} GQA groups \\\\\\\"\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"({pruned_groups * heads_per_kv} Q heads, {pruned_groups} KV heads)\\\\\\\"\\\\n            )\\\\n\\\\n    logger.info(f\\\\\\\"Total attention pruning: {total_pruned_groups} GQA groups removed\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef prune_mlp_channels(model, mlp_keep_masks: dict, n_mlp_groups: int = 28):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Prune MLP channel groups by slicing gate_proj, up_proj, and down_proj.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    config = model.config\\\\n    intermediate_size = config.intermediate_size\\\\n    group_size = intermediate_size // n_mlp_groups\\\\n\\\\n    total_pruned = 0\\\\n\\\\n    for layer_idx, keep_groups in mlp_keep_masks.items():\\\\n        layer = model.m\\\\n\\\\n... Forward pass validation\\n    embed_device = model.model.embed_tokens.weight.device\\n    with torch.no_grad():\\n        dummy_input = torch.randint(0, 1000, (1, 32)).to(embed_device)\\n        try:\\n            output = model(dummy_input)\\n            logger.info(f\\\"Forward pass validation OK, output shape: {output.logits.shape}\\\")\\n        except Exception as e:\\n            logger.error(f\\\"Forward pass FAILED after pruning: {e}\\\")\\n            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Pruned model forward pass failed: {e}\\\")\\n\\n    # {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01HHcA9NxZK4S1eB56fLuw6F","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01J1srSoaDZ5fd5b5SHYAVP3","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westb-23072","command":"cd /root/recovery_aware_struct_prune && source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python3 -c \"\nimport torch\nmeta_std = torch.load('models/pruned_standard_35/pruning_metadata.pt', weights_only=False)\nmeta_rasp = torch.load('models/pruned_rasp_35/pruning_metadata.pt', weights_only=False)\nprint('=== Standard metadata ===')\nfor k, v in meta_std.items():\n    if k in ('attn_keep_masks', 'mlp_keep_masks'):\n        # Show summary\n        if isinstance(v, dict):\n            sizes = {ki: len(vi) for ki, vi in v.items()}\n            print(f'{k}: layer->n_kept = {sizes}')\n        else:\n            print(f'{k}: type={type(v)}')\n    else:\n        print(f'{k}: {v}')\n\nprint()\nprint('=== RASP metadata ===')\nfor k, v in meta_rasp.items():\n    if k in ('attn_keep_masks', 'mlp_keep_masks'):\n        if isinstance(v, dict):\n            sizes = {ki: len(vi) for ki, vi in v.items()}\n            print(f'{k}: layer->n_kept = {sizes}')\n        else:\n            print(f'{k}: type={type(v)}')\n    else:\n        print(f'{k}: {v}')\n\nprint()\nprint('=== Comparing attn_keep_masks ===')\nsame_attn = True\nfor li in range(28):\n    s = sorted(meta_std['attn_keep_masks'].get(li, []))\n    r = sorted(meta_rasp['attn_keep_masks'].get(li, []))\n    if s != r:\n        same_attn = False\n        print(f'Layer {li}: std={s}, rasp={r}')\nif same_attn:\n    print('Attention masks IDENTICAL between standard and RASP')\n\nprint()\nprint('=== Comparing mlp_keep_masks ===')\nsame_mlp = True\nfor li in range(28):\n    s = sorted(meta_std['mlp_keep_masks'].get(li, []))\n    r = sorted(meta_rasp['mlp_keep_masks'].get(li, []))\n    if s != r:\n        same_mlp = False\n        print(f'Layer {li}: std_kept={len(s)}, rasp_kept={len(r)}')\nif same_mlp:\n    print('MLP masks IDENTICAL between standard and RASP')\n\"\n","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":7249,"cache_read_input_tokens":26708,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":7249},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"28278383-e514-450f-b367-078d660b6b05","uuid":"03cd40f5-eec9-4cad-99bf-314db96234d0"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JgiSiGhndUSLGsaTkNFK3z","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nStructured Pruning for Qwen2.5 architecture (GQA-group granularity).\\\\n\\\\nRemoves GQA groups (7 Q heads + 1 KV head each) and MLP channel groups\\\\nbased on RASP or standard importance scores.\\\\n\\\\nInput:  pretrained model + pruning scores\\\\nOutput: pruned model saved to disk\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimport argparse\\\\nimport copy\\\\nimport logging\\\\nimport os\\\\nimport random\\\\n\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nimport torch\\\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\\\n\\\\nlogging.basicConfig(\\\\n    level=logging.INFO,\\\\n    format=\\\\\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\\\\\",\\\\n)\\\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef set_seed(seed: int):\\\\n    random.seed(seed)\\\\n    np.random.seed(seed)\\\\n    torch.manual_seed(seed)\\\\n    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef prune_attention_heads(model, attn_keep_masks: dict):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Prune GQA groups from the model by slicing weight matrices in-place.\\\\n\\\\n    attn_keep_masks: dict[layer_idx -> list of GQA group indices to KEEP]\\\\n    Each GQA group = heads_per_kv Q heads + 1 KV head.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    config = model.config\\\\n    n_heads = config.num_attention_heads\\\\n    n_kv_heads = config.num_key_value_heads\\\\n    head_dim = config.hidden_size // n_heads\\\\n    heads_per_kv = n_heads // n_kv_heads\\\\n\\\\n    total_pruned_groups = 0\\\\n\\\\n    for layer_idx, keep_groups in attn_keep_masks.items():\\\\n        layer = model.model.layers[layer_idx]\\\\n        attn = layer.self_attn\\\\n        n_keep_groups = len(keep_groups)\\\\n        pruned_groups = n_kv_heads - n_keep_groups\\\\n\\\\n        # Expand GQA group indices to Q head weight indices\\\\n        q_indices = []\\\\n        for g in keep_groups:\\\\n            for qh in range(heads_per_kv):\\\\n                h = g * heads_per_kv + qh\\\\n                start = h * head_dim\\\\n                q_indices.extend(range(start, start + head_dim))\\\\n        q_indices = torch.tensor(q_indices, dtype=torch.long)\\\\n\\\\n        # KV head weight indices (1 per group)\\\\n        kv_indices = []\\\\n        for g in keep_groups:\\\\n            start = g * head_dim\\\\n            kv_indices.extend(range(start, start + head_dim))\\\\n        kv_indices = torch.tensor(kv_indices, dtype=torch.long)\\\\n\\\\n        # Slice q_proj: [n_heads*head_dim, hidden] -> [n_keep_q*head_dim, hidden]\\\\n        attn.q_proj.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(attn.q_proj.weight.data[q_indices])\\\\n        if attn.q_proj.bias is not None:\\\\n            attn.q_proj.bias = torch.nn.Parameter(attn.q_proj.bias.data[q_indices])\\\\n\\\\n        # Slice k_proj, v_proj: [n_kv*head_dim, hidden] -> [n_keep_kv*head_dim, hidden]\\\\n        attn.k_proj.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(attn.k_proj.weight.data[kv_indices])\\\\n        if attn.k_proj.bias is not None:\\\\n            attn.k_proj.bias = torch.nn.Parameter(attn.k_proj.bias.data[kv_indices])\\\\n\\\\n        attn.v_proj.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(attn.v_proj.weight.data[kv_indices])\\\\n        if attn.v_proj.bias is not None:\\\\n            attn.v_proj.bias = torch.nn.Parameter(attn.v_proj.bias.data[kv_indices])\\\\n\\\\n        # Slice o_proj: [hidden, n_heads*head_dim] -> [hidden, n_keep_q*head_dim]\\\\n        attn.o_proj.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(attn.o_proj.weight.data[:, q_indices])\\\\n\\\\n        # Update layer config\\\\n        n_keep_q = n_keep_groups * heads_per_kv\\\\n        attn.num_heads = n_keep_q\\\\n        attn.num_key_value_heads = n_keep_groups\\\\n        attn.head_dim = head_dim\\\\n\\\\n        total_pruned_groups += pruned_groups\\\\n\\\\n        if pruned_groups > 0:\\\\n            logger.info(\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"  Layer {layer_idx}: pruned {pruned_groups}/{n_kv_heads} GQA groups \\\\\\\"\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"({pruned_groups * heads_per_kv} Q heads, {pruned_groups} KV heads)\\\\\\\"\\\\n            )\\\\n\\\\n    logger.info(f\\\\\\\"Total attention pruning: {total_pruned_groups} GQA groups removed\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef prune_mlp_channels(model, mlp_keep_masks: dict, n_mlp_groups: int = 28):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Prune MLP channel groups by slicing gate_proj, up_proj, and down_proj.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    config = model.config\\\\n    intermediate_size = config.intermediate_size\\\\n    group_size = intermediate_size // n_mlp_groups\\\\n\\\\n    total_pruned = 0\\\\n\\\\n    for layer_idx, keep_groups in mlp_keep_masks.items():\\\\n        layer = model.model.layers[layer_idx]\\\\n        mlp = layer.mlp\\\\n        n_keep = len(keep_groups)\\\\n        pruned = n_mlp_groups - n_keep\\\\n\\\\n        if pruned == 0:\\\\n            continue\\\\n\\\\n        # Channel indices to keep\\\\n        indices = []\\\\n        for g in keep_groups:\\\\n            start = g * group_size\\\\n            indices.extend(range(start, start + group_size))\\\\n        # Keep remainder channels beyond n_mlp_groups*group_size\\\\n        remainder_start = n_mlp_groups * group_size\\\\n        if remainder_start < intermediate_size:\\\\n            indices.extend(range(remainder_start, intermediate_size))\\\\n        indices = torch.tensor(indices, dtype=torch.long)\\\\n\\\\n        # gate_proj: [intermediate, hidden] -> [n_keep_channels, hidden]\\\\n        mlp.gate_proj.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(mlp.gate_proj.weight.data[indices])\\\\n        if mlp.gate_proj.bias is not None:\\\\n            mlp.gate_proj.bias = torch.nn.Parameter(mlp.gate_proj.bias.data[indices])\\\\n\\\\n        # up_proj: [intermediate, hidden] -> [n_keep_channels, hidden]\\\\n        mlp.up_proj.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(mlp.up_proj.weight.data[indices])\\\\n        if mlp.up_proj.bias is not None:\\\\n            mlp.up_proj.bias = torch.nn.Parameter(mlp.up_proj.bias.data[indices])\\\\n\\\\n        # down_proj: [hidden, intermediate] -> [hidden, n_keep_channels]\\\\n        mlp.down_proj.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(mlp.down_proj.weight.data[:, indices])\\\\n\\\\n        total_pruned += pruned\\\\n        logger.info(\\\\n            f\\\\\\\"  Layer {layer_idx}: pruned {pruned}/{n_mlp_groups} MLP groups \\\\\\\"\\\\n            f\\\\\\\"({pruned * group_size} channels)\\\\\\\"\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n    logger.info(f\\\\\\\"Total MLP pruning: {total_pruned} groups removed\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef count_parameters(model) -> int:\\\\n    return sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef main():\\\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\\\\\"Execute structured pruning on Qwen2.5 model (GQA-group granularity)\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--model_path\\\\\\\", type=str, required=True, help=\\\\\\\"Path to pretrained model\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--importance_path\\\\\\\", type=str, required=True, help=\\\\\\\"Path to importance scores .pt\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--cka_path\\\\\\\", type=str, default=None, help=\\\\\\\"Path to CKA overlap scores .pt\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--mode\\\\\\\", type=str, choices=[\\\\\\\"standard\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"rasp\\\\\\\"], required=True)\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--sparsity\\\\\\\", type=float, default=0.35, help=\\\\\\\"Target sparsity ratio\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--alpha\\\\\\\", type=float, default=1.0, help=\\\\\\\"RASP alpha parameter\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--output_path\\\\\\\", type=str, required=True, help=\\\\\\\"Output path for pruned model\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--seed\\\\\\\", type=int, default=42)\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--dtype\\\\\\\", type=str, default=\\\\\\\"bfloat16\\\\\\\", choices=[\\\\\\\"float16\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"bfloat16\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"float32\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\\\n\\\\n    set_seed(args.seed)\\\\n    device = torch.device(\\\\\\\"cuda\\\\\\\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\")\\\\n    dtype_map = {\\\\\\\"float16\\\\\\\": torch.float16, \\\\\\\"bfloat16\\\\\\\": torch.bfloat16, \\\\\\\"float32\\\\\\\": torch.float32}\\\\n    torch_dtype = dtype_map[args.dtype]\\\\n\\\\n    logger.info(f\\\\\\\"Loading model from {args.model_path}\\\\\\\")\\\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\\\\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\\\n        args.model_path,\\\\n        torch_dtype=torch_dtype,\\\\n        device_map=\\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\",\\\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n    original_params = count_parameters(model)\\\\n    logger.info(f\\\\\\\"Original model parameters: {original_params:,}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    # Load scores\\\\n    importance_data = torch.load(args.importance_path, map_location=\\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\", weights_only=True)\\\\n    attn_importance = importance_data[\\\\\\\"attn_importance\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    mlp_importance = importance_data[\\\\\\\"mlp_importance\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n    if args.mode == \\\\\\\"rasp\\\\\\\":\\\\n        if args.cka_path is None:\\\\n            raise ValueError(\\\\\\\"--cka_path required for rasp mode\\\\\\\")\\\\n        cka_data = torch.load(args.cka_path, map_location=\\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\", weights_only=True)\\\\n        attn_overlap = cka_data[\\\\\\\"attn_overlap\\\\\\\"]\\\\n {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DieZPheDudrZnGLcjbUKKD","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nGSM8K Test Set Evaluation.\\\\n\\\\nEvaluates model accuracy on GSM8K test split by generating reasoning traces\\\\nand comparing extracted numeric answers against gold answers.\\\\n\\\\nInput:  model path\\\\nOutput: accuracy printed to stdout + saved to results JSON\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimport argparse\\\\nimport json\\\\nimport math\\\\nimport logging\\\\nimport os\\\\nimport random\\\\nimport re\\\\n\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nimport torch\\\\nfrom datasets import load_dataset\\\\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer\\\\nfrom model_utils import load_pruned_model\\\\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\\\\n\\\\nlogging.basicConfig(\\\\n    level=logging.INFO,\\\\n    format=\\\\\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\\\\\",\\\\n)\\\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef set_seed(seed: int):\\\\n    random.seed(seed)\\\\n    np.random.seed(seed)\\\\n    torch.manual_seed(seed)\\\\n    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef extract_answer(text: str) -> str | None:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Extract numeric answer from model output.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    boxed = re.findall(r\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\boxed\\\\\\\\{([^}]+)\\\\\\\\}\\\\\\\", text)\\\\n    if boxed:\\\\n        return boxed[-1].strip().replace(\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    hash_match = re.findall(r\\\\\\\"####\\\\\\\\s*(.+)\\\\\\\", text)\\\\n    if hash_match:\\\\n        return hash_match[-1].strip().replace(\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    numbers = re.findall(r\\\\\\\"-?\\\\\\\\d+(?:,\\\\\\\\d{3})*(?:\\\\\\\\.\\\\\\\\d+)?\\\\\\\", text)\\\\n    if numbers:\\\\n        return numbers[-1].replace(\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\")\\\\n    return None\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef normalize_answer(ans: str) -> str | None:\\\\n    ans = ans.strip().replace(\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\").replace(\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\")\\\\n    if not ans:\\\\n        return None\\\\n    try:\\\\n        val = float(ans)\\\\n        if not math.isfinite(val):\\\\n            return None\\\\n        if val == int(val):\\\\n            return str(int(val))\\\\n        return str(val)\\\\n    except (ValueError, OverflowError):\\\\n        return ans\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef extract_gold_answer(answer_text: str) -> str:\\\\n    match = re.search(r\\\\\\\"####\\\\\\\\s*(.+)\\\\\\\", answer_text)\\\\n    if match:\\\\n        result = normalize_answer(match.group(1))\\\\n        return result if result is not None else match.group(1).strip()\\\\n    return answer_text.strip()\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef evaluate(model, tokenizer, dataset, max_new_tokens: int = 512, batch_size: int = 1):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Run evaluation and return accuracy + per-example results.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    model.eval()\\\\n    device = next(model.parameters()).device\\\\n\\\\n    correct = 0\\\\n    total = 0\\\\n    results = []\\\\n\\\\n    for i in tqdm(range(0, len(dataset), batch_size), desc=\\\\\\\"Evaluating\\\\\\\"):\\\\n        batch = dataset[i : i + batch_size]\\\\n        questions = batch[\\\\\\\"question\\\\\\\"] if isinstance(batch[\\\\\\\"question\\\\\\\"], list) else [batch[\\\\\\\"question\\\\\\\"]]\\\\n        answers = batch[\\\\\\\"answer\\\\\\\"] if isinstance(batch[\\\\\\\"answer\\\\\\\"], list) else [batch[\\\\\\\"answer\\\\\\\"]]\\\\n\\\\n        for q, a in zip(questions, answers):\\\\n            prompt = (\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"Solve the following math problem step by step. \\\\\\\"\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"Put your final answer in \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\boxed{{}}.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"Question: {q}\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nAnswer:\\\\\\\"\\\\n            )\\\\n\\\\n            inputs = tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\\\\\"pt\\\\\\\", truncation=True, max_length=1024).to(device)\\\\n\\\\n            with torch.no_grad():\\\\n                output_ids = model.generate(\\\\n                    **inputs,\\\\n                    max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,\\\\n                    temperature=0.0,\\\\n                    do_sample=False,\\\\n                    pad_token_id=tokenizer.pad_token_id or tokenizer.eos_token_id,\\\\n                )\\\\n\\\\n            generated = output_ids[0][inputs[\\\\\\\"input_ids\\\\\\\"].shape[1] :]\\\\n            response = tokenizer.decode(generated, skip_special_tokens=True)\\\\n\\\\n            pred = extract_answer(response)\\\\n            gold = extract_gold_answer(a)\\\\n\\\\n            is_correct = False\\\\n            if pred is not None:\\\\n                norm_pred = normalize_answer(pred)\\\\n                is_correct = norm_pred is not None and norm_pred == gold\\\\n\\\\n            if is_correct:\\\\n                correct += 1\\\\n            total += 1\\\\n\\\\n            results.append({\\\\n                \\\\\\\"question\\\\\\\": q,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"gold\\\\\\\": gold,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"prediction\\\\\\\": pred,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"correct\\\\\\\": is_correct,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"response\\\\\\\": response[:500],  # truncate for storage\\\\n            })\\\\n\\\\n            if total % 100 == 0:\\\\n                logger.info(f\\\\\\\"Progress: {total}/{len(dataset)}, running accuracy: {correct/total:.4f}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    accuracy = correct / total if total > 0 else 0.0\\\\n    return accuracy, results\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef main():\\\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\\\\\"Evaluate model on GSM8K test set\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--model_path\\\\\\\", type=str, required=True, help=\\\\\\\"Path to model\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--output_path\\\\\\\", type=str, default=None, help=\\\\\\\"Output JSON path for results\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--max_new_tokens\\\\\\\", type=int, default=512)\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--max_samples\\\\\\\", type=int, default=None, help=\\\\\\\"Limit test samples (None=all)\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--seed\\\\\\\", type=int, default=42)\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--dtype\\\\\\\", type=str, default=\\\\\\\"bfloat16\\\\\\\", choices=[\\\\\\\"float16\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"bfloat16\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"float32\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\\\n\\\\n    set_seed(args.seed)\\\\n\\\\n    logger.info(f\\\\\\\"Loading model from {args.model_path}\\\\\\\")\\\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\\\\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\\\\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\\\\n\\\\n    dtype_map = {\\\\\\\"float16\\\\\\\": torch.float16, \\\\\\\"bfloat16\\\\\\\": torch.bfloat16, \\\\\\\"float32\\\\\\\": torch.float32}\\\\n    torch_dtype = dtype_map[args.dtype]\\\\n\\\\n    model = load_pruned_model(args.model_path, device_map=\\\\\\\"auto\\\\\\\", torch_dtype=torch_dtype)\\\\n\\\\n    logger.info(\\\\\\\"Loading GSM8K test set...\\\\\\\")\\\\n    dataset = load_dataset(\\\\\\\"openai/gsm8k\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"main\\\\\\\", split=\\\\\\\"test\\\\\\\")\\\\n    if args.max_samples is not None:\\\\n        dataset = dataset.shuffle(seed=args.seed).select(range(min(args.max_samples, len(dataset))))\\\\n    logger.info(f\\\\\\\"Evaluating on {len(dataset)} test samples\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    accuracy, results = evaluate(model, tokenizer, dataset, max_new_tokens=args.max_new_tokens)\\\\n\\\\n    logger.info(f\\\\\\\"GSM8K Accuracy: {accuracy:.4f} ({int(accuracy * len(dataset))}/{len(dataset)})\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    # Determine output path\\\\n    if args.output_path is None:\\\\n        model_name = os.path.basename(args.model_path.rstrip(\\\\\\\"/\\\\\\\"))\\\\n        args.output_path = f\\\\\\\"results/eval_{model_name}.json\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(args.output_path) or \\\\\\\".\\\\\\\", exist_ok=True)\\\\n    output = {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"model_path\\\\\\\": args.model_path,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"accuracy\\\\\\\": accuracy,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"n_correct\\\\\\\": -> str | None:\\n    ans = ans.strip().replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\" \\\", \\\"\\\")\\n    if not ans:\\n        return None\\n    try:\\n        val = float(ans)\\n        if not math.isfinite(val):\\n            return None\\n        if val == int(val):\\n            return str(int(val))\\n        return str(val)\\n    except (ValueError, OverflowError):\\n        return ans\\n\\n\\ndef extract_gold_answer(answer_text: str) -> str:\\n    match = re.search(r\\\"####\\\\s*(.+)\\\", answer_text)\\n    if match:\\n        result = normalize_answer(match.group(1))\\n        return result if result is not None else match.group(1).strip()\\n    return answer_text.strip()\\n\\n\\ndef evaluate(model, tokenizer, dataset, max_new_tokens: int = 512, batch_size: int = 1):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Run evaluation and return accuracy + per-example results.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    model.eval()\\n    device = next(model.parameters()).device\\n\\n    correct = 0\\n    total = 0\\n    results = max_length=1024).to(device)\\n\\n            with torch.no_grad():\\n                output_ids = model.generate(\\n                    **inputs,\\n                    max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,\\n                    temperature=0.0,\\n                    do_sample=False,\\n                    pad_token_id=tokenizer.pad_token_id or tokenizer.eos_token_id,\\n                )\\n\\n            generated = output_ids[0][inputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"].shape[1] :]\\n            response = tokenizer.decode(generated, skip_special_tokens=True)\\n\\n            pred = extract_answer(response)\\n            gold = extract_gold_answer(a)\\n\\n            is_correct = False\\n            if pred is not None:\\n                norm_pred = normalize_answer(pred)\\n response[:500],  # truncate for storage\\n            })\\n\\n            if total % 100 == 0:\\n                logger.info(f\\\"Progress: {total}/{len(dataset)}, running accuracy: \\\"import logging\\\\nimport os\\\\n\\\\nimport torch\\\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\\\nfrom transformers import AutoConfig, AutoModelForCausalLM\\\\n\\\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef _load_state_dict(model_path):\\\\n    safetensor_files = sorted(\\\\n        f for f in os.listdir(model_path) if f.endswith(\\\\\\\".safetensors\\\\\\\")\\\\n    )\\\\n    if safetensor_files:\\\\n        from safetensors.torch import load_file\\\\n        state_dict = {}\\\\n        for f in safetensor_files:\\\\n            state_dict.update(load_file(os.path.join(model_path, f)))\\\\n        return state_dict\\\\n    return torch.load(\\\\n        os.path.join(model_path, \\\\\\\"pytorch_model.bin\\\\\\\"),\\\\n        map_location=\\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\", weights_only=True,\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef load_pruned_model(model_path, device_map=\\\\\\\"auto\\\\\\\", torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Load a pruned model that may have non-uniform MLP/attention dimensions per layer.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    config = AutoConfig.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\\\\n    per_layer_sizes = getattr(config, \\\\\\\"per_layer_intermediate_size\\\\\\\", None)\\\\n    per_layer_heads = getattr(config, \\\\\\\"per_layer_num_heads\\\\\\\", None)\\\\n    per_layer_kv_heads = getattr(config, \\\\\\\"per_layer_num_kv_heads\\\\\\\", None)\\\\n\\\\n    if per_layer_sizes is None and per_layer_heads is None:\\\\n        return AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\\\n            model_path, torch_dtype=torch_dtype, device_map=device_map,\\\\n            trust_remote_code=True,\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n    hidden_size = config.hidden_size\\\\n    head_dim = getattr(config, \\\\\\\"head_dim\\\\\\\", hidden_size // config.num_attention_heads)\\\\n\\\\n    if per_layer_sizes is not None:\\\\n        max_size = max(per_layer_sizes)\\\\n        config.intermediate_size = max_size\\\\n        logger.info(\\\\n            \\\\\\\"Non-uniform MLP detected: intermediate_size range [%d, %d]\\\\\\\",\\\\n            min(per_layer_sizes), max_size,\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n    if per_layer_heads is not None:\\\\n        config.num_attention_heads = max(per_layer_heads)\\\\n        config.num_key_value_heads = max(per_layer_kv_heads)\\\\n        logger.info(\\\\n            \\\\\\\"Non-uniform attention detected: heads range [%d, %d], kv_heads range [%d, %d]\\\\\\\",\\\\n            min(per_layer_heads), max(per_layer_heads),\\\\n            min(per_layer_kv_heads), max(per_layer_kv_heads),\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n    prev_dtype = torch.get_default_dtype()\\\\n    torch.set_default_dtype(torch_dtype)\\\\n    try:\\\\n        model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_config(config)\\\\n        num_layers = len(model.model.layers)\\\\n\\\\n        for layer_idx in range(num_layers):\\\\n            layer = model.model.layers[layer_idx]\\\\n\\\\n            if per_layer_sizes is not None:\\\\n                size = per_layer_sizes[layer_idx]\\\\n                if size != config.intermediate_size:\\\\n                    mlp = layer.mlp\\\\n                    mlp.gate_proj = nn.Linear(hidden_size, size, bias=False)\\\\n                    mlp.up_proj = nn.Linear(hidden_size, size, bias=False)\\\\n                    mlp.down_proj = nn.Linear(size, hidden_size, bias=False)\\\\n\\\\n            if per_layer_heads is not None:\\\\n                nh = per_layer_heads[layer_idx]\\\\n                nkv = per_layer_kv_heads[layer_idx]\\\\n                if nh != config.num_attention_heads or nkv != config.num_key_value_heads:\\\\n                    attn = layer.self_attn\\\\n                    q_out = nh * head_dim\\\\n                    kv_out = nkv * head_dim\\\\n                    attn.q_proj = nn.Linear(hidden_size, q_out, bias=getattr(attn.q_proj, 'bias', None) is not None)\\\\n                    attn.k_proj = nn.Linear(hidden_size, kv_out, bias=getattr(attn.k_proj, 'bias', None) is not None)\\\\n                    attn.v_proj = nn.Linear(hidden_size, kv_out, bias=getattr(attn.v_proj, 'bias', None) is not None)\\\\n                    attn.o_proj = nn.Linear(q_out, hidden_size, bias=getattr(attn.o_proj, 'bias', None) is not None)\\\\n                    attn.num_heads = nh\\\\n                    attn.num_key_value_heads = nkv\\\\n                    attn.num_key_value_groups = max(1, nh // max(1, nkv))\\\\n                    attn.head_dim = head_dim\\\\n    finally:\\\\n        torch.set_default_dtype(prev_dtype)\\\\n\\\\n    state_dict = _load_state_dict(model_path)\\\\n    model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=True)\\\\n    del state_dict\\\\n\\\\n    if device_map == \\\\\\\"auto\\\\\\\":\\\\n        model = model.cuda()\\\\n    elif device_map is not None and str(device_map) != \\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\":\\\\n        model = model.to(device_map)\\\\n\\\\n    model.eval()\\\\n    return model\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, How many does she sell in 4 days?\\nAnswer:\"\n    inputs = tokenizer(text, return_tensors=\"pt\").to(device)\n    \n    with torch.no_grad():\n        outputs = model(**inputs, output_hidden_states=True)\n    \n    for i, hs in enumerate(outputs.hidden_states):\n        norm = hs.float().norm().item()\n        has_nan = torch.isnan(hs).any().item()\n        has_inf = torch.isinf(hs).any().item()\n        mean_abs = hs.float().abs().mean().item()\n        print(f\"Layer {i:2d}: norm={norm:10.2f}, mean_abs={mean_abs:.6f}, nan={has_nan}, inf={has_inf}\")\n    \n    # Generation test\n    print(\"\\n--- Generation test (3 samples) ---\")\n    prompts = [\n        \"Question: Janet sells 16 eggs per day.
